    E33 · app/server/knowledge-service.ts:86–99

    requirePolicy 授权复合动作，取 conditions.database 的 policy，deny 或无 policy 时抛 AuthorizationDeniedError。

    async function requirePolicy(
  context: AuthorizationContext,
  action: 'read' | 'manage',
): Promise<RepositoryPolicy> {
  const decision = await context.authorize({
    resource: { type: 'composite', id: KNOWLEDGE_DOCUMENTS_RESOURCE },
    action,
  });
  const policy = policyFromDecision(decision);
  if (decision.effect === 'deny' || !policy) {
    throw new AuthorizationDeniedError(decision);
  }
  return policy;
}

    原文件 SHA-256：5b1824e6521cda9b1136228237f5a59173dd1da740590d9a270b7ce0f6fc0d81

    E34 · app/server/knowledge-service.ts:178–244

    list/get/update/search 全部通过 documents().withPolicy(policy) 访问，受限记录在打分前即被 SQL 层排除。

      const documents = (): Repository<KnowledgeDocument> =>
    database.repository<KnowledgeDocument>(KNOWLEDGE_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION);

  // A Repository Policy makes every field the grant did not list optional in
  // the type. The grants this feature checks list the whole row, so the cast
  // is the truthful shape; the policy still constrains the SQL and the
  // writable fields at run time.
  const asDocument = (row: Partial<KnowledgeDocument> | undefined) =>
    row as KnowledgeDocument | undefined;
  const asDocuments = (rows: Partial<KnowledgeDocument>[]) =>
    rows as KnowledgeDocument[];

  return {
    async contextForActor(
      actor: KnowledgeActor,
    ): Promise<AuthorizationContext> {
      const principal = { type: 'user', id: String(actor.id) };
      const subjects = [
        { type: 'authenticated', id: '*' },
        ...(await authorization.subjects.resolveFor(principal)),
      ];
      return authorization.for({ principal, subjects });
    },

    async listDocuments(context) {
      const policy = await requirePolicy(context, 'read');
      return asDocuments(await documents().withPolicy(policy).findMany());
    },

    async getDocument(context, documentId) {
      const policy = await requirePolicy(context, 'read');
      return asDocument(
        await documents()
          .withPolicy(policy)
          .findOne({ filter: { id: documentId } }),
      );
    },

    async updateDocument(context, documentId, input) {
      const policy = await requirePolicy(context, 'manage');
      const repository = documents().withPolicy(policy);
      const existing = await repository.findOne({ filter: { id: documentId } });
      if (!existing) {
        return undefined;
      }
      const values: Partial<KnowledgeDocument> = { updatedAt: new Date() };
      if (input.title !== undefined) {
        values.title = input.title;
      }
      if (input.body !== undefined) {
        values.body = input.body;
      }
      await repository.updateOne({ filter: { id: documentId }, values });
      return asDocument(
        await repository.findOne({ filter: { id: documentId } }),
      );
    },

    async searchDocuments(context, query, limit = 5) {
      const policy = await requirePolicy(context, 'read');
      const visible = asDocuments(
        await documents().withPolicy(policy).findMany(),
      );
      return rankDocuments(visible, query).slice(0, limit);
    },
  };
}

    原文件 SHA-256：5b1824e6521cda9b1136228237f5a59173dd1da740590d9a270b7ce0f6fc0d81

    E35 · app/server/providers/knowledge.ts:31–80

    provider 在 register 绑定 knowledge service，在 boot 依次注册集合/记录访问/复合资源与 AI 资源，且以 container.has 守卫可选依赖。

    export default class KnowledgeProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name = 'app/knowledge';

  public override register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(knowledgeServiceToken, (resolver) =>
      createKnowledgeService(
        resolver.resolve(databaseManagerToken),
        resolver.resolve(authorizationToken),
      ),
    );
  }

  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {
    const { container } = this.app;

    // Both dependencies are checked by registration rather than resolved
    // unconditionally: a composition that omits the Authorization or AI
    // Employee plugin still boots this App's providers, and mounting the
    // knowledge routes without Authorization still fails loudly when the route
    // factory resolves the token at registration time.
    if (container.has(authorizationToken)) {
      const authz = container.resolve(authorizationToken);

      // Opt the collection into the permission model. Every action the feature
      // checks has to be registered here, including for the root user.
      authz.database.collections.add({
        name: KNOWLEDGE_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION,
        title: { key: 'knowledge.collection.documents', ns: 'nb3-factory' },
      });

      // The named record access must exist before the composite that offers it,
      // because `define` validates each data scope against the offered options.
      authz.recordAccess.define(publicDocuments);
      const reference = authz.compositeResources.define(knowledgeDocuments);

      authz.ui.sections.add({
        name: 'knowledge',
        title: { key: 'knowledge.section', ns: 'nb3-factory' },
        parent: 'business',
      });
      authz.ui.place(reference, { section: 'knowledge' });
    }

    if (container.has(aiManagerToken)) {
      const ai = container.resolve(aiManagerToken);
      await new KnowledgeAIResources({
        source: 'application',
      }).registerAIResources(ai);
    }
  }

Do not modify the plugin's own source,\nmigrations, or `dist/`; everything below is done in the application.\n\nThe plugin wraps Better Auth. Application configuration is Better Auth\nconfiguration, and every Better Auth plugin, social provider, and hook is\navailable through it. Read the installed Better Auth version's documentation\nfor option details; this Skill covers where those options go in a NocoBase\napplication and what the plugin adds on top.\n\n## Public surfaces\n\nServer, from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication` (the root entry is the\nserver entry) or `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/server`:\n\n- `authenticationToken` resolves the `Auth` instance: `required()`,\n  `optional()`, `getSession(headers)`, `handler(request)`.\n- `AuthEnv` types a Hono router whose routes read `context.get('auth')`.\n- `userAdministrationServiceToken` resolves `UserAdministrationService`:\n  `list`, `get`, `create`, `update`, `disable`, `enable`, `resetPassword`,\n  `revokeSessions`, `withConnection`; errors are `UserAdministrationError`.\n- `AuthConfig` is Better Auth's `BetterAuthOptions`, the type of the application's `server/config/auth.ts`. User initialization configuration lives separately under `users.initialAdmin`.\n- `createAuthentication`, `databaseAdapter`, `createAuthStorage` build an\n  instance outside the application runtime, mainly in tests.\n\nClient, from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/client`:\n\n- `authentication()` is the registration factory. It takes no options,\n  contributes no routes, and mounts one React provider.\n- `useAuthentication()` returns `{ client, session, isPending, refresh }`;\n  `useAuthenticationClient()` returns the Better Auth client alone.\n- `RequiredAuthentication`, `GuestAuthentication`, and\n  `AuthenticationGuard({ mode })` gate a route subtree on the session.\n- `AuthConfig` is Better Auth's client options, the type of the application's\n  `client/config/auth.ts`.\n- `authenticationClientToken` is the client's service token, used to stub the\n  client in component tests.\n\nHeadless actions, from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/client/actions`:\n`usePasswordLogin`, `usePasswordRegistration`, `usePasswordResetRequest`,\n`usePasswordReset`, each returning `{ submit, isPending, error }`.\n\nHTTP: every Better Auth endpoint is served under `/api/auth/*` by the plugin's\nown route. A Better Auth plugin's endpoints appear there automatically. There\nis no other authentication REST surface.\n\nAPI document: the plugin lets a signed-in session read the App's OpenAPI document at `GET /api/swagger` and its Swagger UI at `GET /api/swagger/docs`; an anonymous request gets `401` with `error.reason` `API_DOCS_UNAUTHENTICATED`, and `@nocobase/app-plugin-api-keys` adds API keys as a second way in. The plugin also merges Better Auth's endpoints into that document from Better Auth's own OpenAPI generator, at their full `/api/auth/...` paths and tagged `Authentication`, so a Better Auth plugin added to `auth.plugins` is documented without further work. Browser-only steps are left out: `/sign-in/social`, `/link-social`, `/callback/{id}`, `/verify-email`, `/reset-password/{token}`, `/delete-user/callback` and `/error`. Do not add Better Auth's `openAPI()` plugin: it would publish its own unauthenticated `/api/auth/reference` page and schema route.\n\n## Choose the task path\n\n| The task is                                                                                  | Read                                                                         |\n| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| Require or read the session in an API route, compose with authorization, test it             | [protecting routes](references/protecting-routes.md)                         |\n| Read the user in a component, sign out, gate a page, change the login pages, add SSO buttons | [client session and pages](references/client-session-and-pages.md)           |\n| Add GitHub, Google, OIDC, magic link, or another sign-in method                              | [adding sign-in methods](references/adding-sign-in-methods.md)               |\n| The identity platform uses a protocol Better Auth cannot express                             | [custom Better Auth plugin](references/custom-better-auth-plugin.md)         |\n| Disable or reset an account from server code, add user fields, send reset emails, deploy     | [user lifecycle and deployment](references/user-lifecycle-and-deployment.md) |\n\nRead only the reference the task needs.\n\n## Ownership\n\n- The plugin owns the protocol, the `user`, `session`, `account`, and\n  `verification` collections and their migrations, session validation, the\n  guards, the headless actions, and the `/api/auth/*` route.\n- The application owns `server/config/auth.ts`, `client/config/auth.ts`, the\n  four guest routes in `client/routes.ts`, the pages in `client/pages/auth/`,\n  the authentication UI in `client/extensions/nocobase-auth-*/`, its own migrations for any\n  schema a sign-in method adds, and every environment variable and secret.\n- Authorization owns permissions. Users owns the administration page. The\n  application's own code decides which roles exist.\n- The plugin's `skills/` source is authoritative. `.agents/skills/` is a\n  synchronized copy and must not be edited.\n\n## Reversible UI customization\n\nPrefer props and page composition in `client/pages/auth/`, then new application components, over editing the UI Library blocks in `client/extensions/nocobase-auth-*/`. Reuse the headless authentication actions in custom forms. Disabling registration or another feature should preserve its pages and components, conditionally disable the route, and hide its entry points so it can be restored. The server must still reject the disabled operation. Read [client session and pages](references/client-session-and-pages.md) for the implementation and verification rules.\n\n## Constraints\n\n- Mounting under `/api` authenticates nothing. A route is protected only by\n  `auth.required()` on it. `auth.optional()` never rejects.\n- Browser guards are navigation, not security. The server authenticates every\n  request independently.\n- Configuring a Better Auth plugin changes no database. Any model or field it\n  needs is an application migration in `database/migrations/`; never copy or\n  edit the plugin's migrations, and never let Better Auth alter tables.\n- Sessions and cookies are created by Better Auth only. Do not mint a second\n  token, store a session in `localStorage`, or resolve identity from a client\n  claim.\n- Bind an external identity by a stable `issuer + subject`, never by an\n  unverified email. Merging accounts on email is a product decision that has to\n  be stated explicitly before it is implemented.\n- Secrets stay in server configuration or environment. Nothing under `client/`\n  may read one.\n- Never log a password, session token, verification value, ticket, or OAuth\n  token. Log user ids, request ids, and outcomes.\n- A disabled account is rejected at sign-in with `403 ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, and\n  its existing sessions and realtime connections are revoked at once. Do not\n  add a parallel \"active\" flag.\n\n## Verification\n\n- Anonymous requests to a protected route return `401` with the standard error body, `error.reason` `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED`; the same request with a session returns the route's own response.\n- An authenticated request that lacks permission returns `403` from\n  authorization, not `401`.\n- A `required` route redirects an anonymous browser to `/login`; a `guest`\n  route redirects a signed-in browser to `/`.\n- After sign-out the previous cookie no longer authenticates.\n- A new sign-in method signs a new user in, signs an already bound user in,\n  rejects an invalid, expired, or reused credential, and creates exactly one\n  binding under concurrent first sign-ins.\n- A migration added for a sign-in method runs `up` and `down` against a real\n  test database.\n- The application passes `lint`, `typecheck`, `test`, and `build`. Skill\n  synchronization alone proves only that the copy matches this source.\n\n## Trusted plugin API\n\n`Auth.pluginApi<TPlugin>(pluginId)` returns only the registered plugin’s API methods through Better Auth’s normal dispatch, including before and after hooks. It does not expose the full authentication context and does not authenticate a caller. `Auth.forConnection(connection)` binds those operations to a caller-owned transaction. Consumers still enforce authorization; HTTP-specific hooks must explicitly check for `request` or `headers` instead of accidentally denying trusted server calls.\n\n## Initial administrator\n\nBefore the first seed run, set `users.initialAdmin.username`, `users.initialAdmin.email` and `users.initialAdmin.password` in application configuration to customize the bootstrap account. Omitting the entire node preserves `nocobase` / `admin@nocobase.com` / `admin123`; an explicit node requires a nonempty password and defaults the username to `nocobase` and the email to `admin@nocobase.com`. Usernames contain 3–30 letters, digits, underscores or dots, emails must be valid addresses, and both are stored lowercase. Initialization only runs when the user table is empty, hashes the password and grants the newly created account root permission through the authorization plugin. After initialization, configuration changes do not reset accounts or rerun the recorded seeds. The root permission seed selects the administrator by the same configured username. Use the user administration service for subsequent account changes. Do not edit historical seeds or add a competing administrator seed.\n"}],"details":{}}}

Use for any task under database/ or any server code that touches data. Do not use for the NocoBase 2 @nocobase/database package.'\nmetadata:\n  short-description: Configure, migrate and query the database in a NocoBase 3 application\n---\n\n# The database in a NocoBase 3 application\n\nMost mistakes here are made before the first line of code, by putting a task at the wrong layer: changing schema from runtime code, reading records through the schema builder, or reimplementing relation writes on top of raw queries.\n\nSix areas, in the order an application grows into them:\n\n| Area                                            | Owns                                                         |\n| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |\n| [Configuration](#1-configuration)               | which databases exist, and which dialect each one speaks     |\n| [Migrations and seeds](#2-migrations-and-seeds) | schema history and install data                              |\n| [Builder](#3-builder)                           | how a migration declares tables, fields and constraints      |\n| [Repository and Query](#4-repository-and-query) | runtime reads and writes                                     |\n| [Transactions](#5-transactions)                 | making several writes succeed or fail together               |\n| [Collections](#6-collections)                   | what the database actually resolves to, for code and for you |\n\nThe exact parameters and return types are always the TypeScript declarations in `@nocobase/db`. This Skill decides which API to reach for, where the code belongs, and the boundaries the types cannot express.\n\n## Where the code lives\n\n```text\nserver/config/database.ts          connections and the default connection\nconfig.yml                         per-environment overrides and credentials (gitignored)\ndatabase/<connection>/migrations   schema history for that connection\ndatabase/<connection>/seeds        install data for that connection\ndatabase/<connection>/collections  resolved Collection artifacts\nserver/routes/, server/providers   runtime reads and writes\n```\n\n`<connection>` is a connection name from `server/config/database.ts`, so the default connection's migrations are in `database/main/migrations/`. Adding a connection means adding its directory alongside and keeping `database/tsconfig.json` in place, so migrations and seeds are typechecked and linted as server source.\n\nRuntime code never changes schema, and a migration never imports runtime code. That split is what these directories encode.\n\n## 1. Configuration\n\nConnections are declared in code and overridden per environment:\n\n| Location                    | Responsibility                                                        |\n| --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `server/config/database.ts` | Connection defaults through `defineAppDatabaseConfig`                 |\n| `config.yml`                | Environment overrides including credentials — gitignored              |\n| `config.example.yml`        | The documented structure and credential placeholders; keep it in step |\n\n```ts\nimport { defineAppDatabaseConfig } from '@nocobase/app-server/database';\n\nexport default defineAppDatabaseConfig(({ paths }) => ({\n  default: 'main',\n  connections: {\n    main: {\n      dialect: 'sqlite',\n      filename: paths.storage('database.sqlite'),\n      schemaManagement: 'managed',\n      debug: false,\n    },\n  },\n}));\n```\n\nThe dialects are `sqlite`, `postgres`, `mysql`, `mssql`, `oracle`, `dameng`, `kingbase` and `oceanbase`. Each is an optional peer of `@nocobase/app-server` shipped as its own package: install `@nocobase/db-<dialect>` into `dependencies` — so it reaches the deployed server — and configure the dialect. No import or driver registration is needed, and the dialect package brings its own underlying driver.\n\nSwitching a dialect means changing **both** layers. `config.yml` deep-merges into the code defaults, so editing only the code leaves a stale YAML override in place — with fields belonging to the previous dialect still applied. Remove `filename` from a connection switched away from SQLite. In YAML, `database:` is an alias for `filename` and takes precedence over it, which is the usual reason a changed `filename` appears to do nothing.\n\n`schemaManagement` decides who owns the schema. A connection the application owns is `'managed'` and has migrations; a database owned by another system is `'external'`, which rejects builder DDL and migrations while still allowing record reads and writes, subject to the database account's own permissions. `pnpm nocobase db apply --all` reports external connections as skipped, and targeting one explicitly is an error.\n\nPassing `pnpm typecheck` validates neither the YAML contents nor connectivity. Only starting the application against the target proves either.\n\nThe mechanics belong to the application rather than to this package: how `pnpm nocobase config init` configures one, the per-dialect connection fields and defaults, driver installation and native binaries, and the typing of `defineAppDatabaseConfig` are in `.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/database-connections.md`.\n\nKeep `@nocobase/db` itself in `dependencies`, not `devDependencies`: the deployed server resolves it at runtime, and TypeScript reads that declaration to infer the database configuration.\n\n## 2. Migrations and seeds\n\nA migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job, and a seed is never sample or demonstration content.\n\n```ts\n// database/main/migrations/202609020001_create_orders.ts\nimport { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';\n\nconst migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({\n  name: '202609020001_create_orders',\n\n  async up({ builder }) {\n    await builder.createCollection('orders', (collection) => {\n      collection.increments('id');\n      collection.string('orderNo', { length: 64, nullable: false });\n      collection.string('status', { length: 32, nullable: false });\n      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });\n      collection.unique('orderNo');\n      collection.index('status');\n    });\n  },\n\n  async down({ builder }) {\n    await builder.dropCollection('orders');\n  },\n});\n\nexport default migration;\n```\n\n```ts\n// database/main/seeds/202609030002_default_order_statuses.ts\nimport { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';\n\nexport default defineSeed({\n  name: '202609030002_default_order_statuses',\n\n  async run({ repository }) {\n    await repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne({\n      filter: { code: 'draft' },\n      create: { code: 'draft', title: 'Draft' },\n      update: { title: 'Draft' },\n    });\n  },\n});\n```\n\nApply both with `pnpm nocobase db apply`, adding `--connection <name>` for a non-default connection or `--all` for every managed one.\n\nEach context carries its own tools, and which one to use is not a free choice — see [Repository and Query](#4-repository-and-query). In short: a migration works through `builder` and `query`, and a seed writes through `repository`.\n\nThe rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:\n\n- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.\n- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.\n- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.\n- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.\n- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.\n\nA backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.\n\nBoth run inside a transaction by default. `transaction: false` opts out, and a failure then leaves partial work behind with no history record, so take it only when the operation genuinely cannot run in one.\n\nA task's `connection` is not the runtime connection either. It carries `dialect`, `capabilities` and `client()` — enough to branch on the database, and deliberately not a way back to the manager. Anything conditional on the dialect reads it from there.\n\n## 3. Builder\n\n`builder` is the schema API, and a migration is where it belongs. Runtime code has `db.builder()` available and should not use it: schema that changes outside the migration history is schema nothing can reproduce.\n\n`createCollection(name, callback)` takes a callback, not a field array:\n\n```ts\nawait builder.createCollection('orderItems', (collection) => {\n  collection.increments('id');\n  collection.integer('orderId', { nullable: false });\n  collection

    E61 · app/database/main/seeds/202610100004_knowledge_permissions.ts:13–49

    应用 seed 定义 knowledge-supervisor（allRecords）与 knowledge-colleague（publicDocuments）两个权限集并把 grant 绑到命名记录访问。

    /**
 * The two business permission sets and their assignments.
 *
 * A set carries the same grants the UI and the assistant check:
 *
 *   knowledge-supervisor  read + manage every document, both pages
 *   knowledge-colleague   read only the public documents, both pages
 *
 * Neither set is `root`, so neither bypasses authorization. The platform's
 * `member` set stays untouched: it is assigned to every authenticated subject
 * by its own seed and carries no grants.
 */
const pageGrant = (id: string): PermissionGrant => ({
  resource: { type: 'page', id },
  actions: [{ action: 'access' }],
});

const readAll = { documents: 'allRecords' } as const;
const readPublic = { documents: PUBLIC_DOCUMENTS_ACCESS } as const;

const supervisorSet = definePermissionSet('knowledge-supervisor')
  .title({ key: 'knowledge.permissionSet.supervisor', ns: 'nb3-factory' })
  .grant(
    knowledgeDocuments.reference().grant({ read: readAll, manage: readAll }),
    pageGrant(KNOWLEDGE_PAGES.assistant),
    pageGrant(KNOWLEDGE_PAGES.documents),
  )
  .build();

const colleagueSet = definePermissionSet('knowledge-colleague')
  .title({ key: 'knowledge.permissionSet.colleague', ns: 'nb3-factory' })
  .grant(
    knowledgeDocuments.reference().grant({ read: readPublic }),
    pageGrant(KNOWLEDGE_PAGES.assistant),
    pageGrant(KNOWLEDGE_PAGES.documents),
  )
  .build();

    原文件 SHA-256：382e94bea3d8d99f5e0d678352a35ec5c5ebe2c7b87afbedfbe1e03c338bcdaa

    E62 · app/server/knowledge-resources.ts:45–62

    应用用 defineRecordAccess + condition(visibility=public) 声明可复用记录访问，受限资料对基于该访问的 policy 不可见。

    /**
 * Documents a reader may see: everything marked `public`. A restricted document
 * is invisible to any policy built on this access, which is also what keeps it
 * out of the assistant's retrieval — the filter is applied before scoring.
 */
export const publicDocuments = defineRecordAccess(
  PUBLIC_DOCUMENTS_ACCESS,
  (access) =>
    access
      .title({
        key: 'knowledge.recordAccess.publicDocuments',
        ns: 'nb3-factory',
      })
      .collections(KNOWLEDGE_DOCUMENTS_COLLECTION)
      .resolver(() =>
        condition('visibility', '$eq', DOCUMENT_VISIBILITY.public),
      ),
);

    原文件 SHA-256：93b5279c268a4afcb564801dcbf0f7394bf18d1aec9c533ea718dd6e97947412

    E63 · app/server/knowledge-resources.ts:99–129

    应用用 defineCompositeResource 声明 read/manage 两个动作，分别绑定文档读/管数据库权限与数据范围选项。

    /**
 * The composite the document endpoints authorize against, and the assistant
 * tool reuses the same grant through its own authorization context. Declared
 * after the record access it references, so registration validates cleanly.
 */
export const knowledgeDocuments = defineCompositeResource(
  KNOWLEDGE_DOCUMENTS_RESOURCE,
  (resource) =>
    resource
      .title({ key: 'knowledge.resource.documents', ns: 'nb3-factory' })
      .action('read', (action) =>
        action
          .title({ key: 'knowledge.action.documents.read', ns: 'nb3-factory' })
          .grant('documents', documentReadData, {
            title: { key: 'knowledge.data.documents.read', ns: 'nb3-factory' },
          }),
      )
      .action('manage', (action) =>
        action
          .title({
            key: 'knowledge.action.documents.manage',
            ns: 'nb3-factory',
          })
          .grant('documents', documentManageData, {
            title: {
              key: 'knowledge.data.documents.manage',
              ns: 'nb3-factory',
            },
          }),
      ),
);

    原文件 SHA-256：93b5279c268a4afcb564801dcbf0f7394bf18d1aec9c533ea718dd6e97947412

    E64 · app/database/main/migrations/202610100001_create_knowledge_documents.ts:3–26

    应用迁移用 builder.createCollection 建 knowledgeDocuments 表，含 title/body/visibility/timestamps 与唯一标题约束，并有 down 回滚。

    // The internal document library behind the read-only "资料助手". Only the two
// user-visible fields (title, body) plus the visibility flag the permission
// layer reads are stored; the assistant never writes anything else.
const migration = defineMigration({
  name: '202610100001_create_knowledge_documents',
  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('knowledgeDocuments', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('title', { length: 255 }).notNull();
      collection.text('body').notNull();
      // 'public' | 'restricted'. `restricted` documents are supervisor-only and
      // are filtered out of every query made under a colleague's policy.
      collection.string('visibility', { length: 32 }).notNull();
      collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull();
      collection.datetime('updatedAt').notNull();
      collection.unique('title', { name: 'uq_knowledge_documents_title' });
    });
  },
  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('knowledgeDocuments');
  },
});

export default migration;

    01

    不依赖 AI 服务即可验收：主管维护三条资料，普通同事只能阅读 A/B，C 的页面及直接读取接口均拒绝普通同事访问；刷新后资料仍保存。未配置模型时助手显示明确未配置状态，资料页仍可用。 以下 B01–B05 在独立业务测试模型配置可用后执行；缺模型时逐项记 blocked，继续完成资料、权限、助手接入和未配置状态的搭建，不用固定回复替代真实调用。

    A1 · 5 条操作 · 7 条观察 · 9 张证据
    Agent 报告通过
    ⌄

    原始验收要求

    不依赖 AI 服务即可验收：主管维护三条资料，普通同事只能阅读 A/B，C 的页面及直接读取接口均拒绝普通同事访问；刷新后资料仍保存。未配置模型时助手显示明确未配置状态，资料页仍可用。 以下 B01–B05 在独立业务测试模型配置可用后执行；缺模型时逐项记 blocked，继续完成资料、权限、助手接入和未配置状态的搭建，不用固定回复替代真实调用。

    实际操作

    1. 以超级管理员 nocobase 登录 http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/ ，打开 Internal knowledge > Documents，确认三条资料存在：A 蓝鹭设备报修电话 400-000-7316、B 蓝鹭设备常规巡检间隔 45 天、C 保密项目内部代号 墨竹 729（Supervisor only）。
    2. 在 B 的 Edit 弹窗核对原值回填（标题与正文均为原内容、45 天），将正文改为 60 天并 Save；刷新页面 B 仍为 60 天，随后改回 45 天再次 Save。
    3. 退出后以普通同事 knowledge.colleague / KnowledgeColleague1! 登录，打开 Internal knowledge > Documents，并用当前浏览器会话请求 GET /main/api/knowledge/documents?pageSize=50 、GET /main/api/knowledge/documents/3 、PUT /main/api/knowledge/documents/2 与 POST /main/api/knowledge/documents 。
    4. 以主管 knowledge.supervisor / KnowledgeSupervisor1! 登录，打开 Documents 查看三条资料，编辑 B 为 60 天后刷新，再用会话请求 GET /main/api/knowledge/documents/3 与列表接口，最后把 B 改回 45 天。
    5. 在普通同事与主管身份分别打开 Document assistant，查看助手状态与 Open the documents 链接。

    观察结果

    1. 管理员/主管的 Documents 页面显示全部三条资料，正文分别为 400-000-7316、45 天、墨竹 729，C 标注 Supervisor only。
    2. 编辑弹窗正确回填原标题与正文；保存 60 天后页面显示 60 天，浏览器刷新后仍为 60 天（Last updated 变化），说明修改被持久化；再改回 45 天成功。
    3. 普通同事的 Documents 页面只显示 A、B 两条，无 Edit 按钮，C 完全不可见。
    4. 普通同事直接读取接口 GET /main/api/knowledge/documents?pageSize=50 返回 total=2（仅 id 1、2），不含 C；GET /main/api/knowledge/documents/3 返回 404 KNOWLEDGE_DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND 'Document 3 was not found.'；PUT/POST 写入路由均返回 404 ROUTE_NOT_FOUND，无法修改或新建资料。
    5. 主管的 Documents 页面显示三条资料且都有 Edit 按钮；主管 GET /main/api/knowledge/documents/3 返回 200 且包含 C 正文，列表 total=3，证实限制是基于角色的。
    6. 普通同事与主管打开 Document assistant 均显示 'The assistant is unavailable / No AI model is enabled, so the assistant cannot answer right now. You can still read the documents yourself.'，且 Open the documents / Documents 页面仍可正常打开。
    7. 页面与接口调用过程中未出现 Something went wrong、空白页或非预期 5xx。

    来源：verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed
